10	## PL-01: Method check
11	**Entry:** `src/rllab/train/pipelines/pl01_check.py::run_check` · **Trigger:** `rllab check <config.toml>`; also PL-03 rows 2–5
12	**Covers:** TRAIN-06
13	
14	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
15	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
16	| 1 | `load_run_configs` | train.config | config path, seeds → list[RunConfig] | `tomllib` into frozen dataclasses, method defaults filled, name `<stem>-s<seed>` | TRAIN-01 |
17	| 2 | `check_plan` | train.check | list[RunConfig] → MethodConfig per method | each distinct team method with `check_settings` applied | TRAIN-06 |
18	| 3 | `train_checks` | train.check | MethodConfig per method, seed → Learner per method | make the method's check world and learner, step it for the fixed check budget | TRAIN-06 |
19	| 4 | `CheckDiscrete` | worlds.checks | overrides → CheckWorld | 6-state corridor, exits left of 0 (0.8) and right of 5 (1.0), γ = 0.9 (AD-09) | TRAIN-06 |
20	| 5 | `CheckContinuous` | worlds.checks | overrides → CheckWorld | 1-D point, reward −distance to the target | TRAIN-06 |
21	| 6 | `score_checks` | train.check | Learner per method, MethodConfig per method → list[CheckResult] | share of `states()` where `policy().act` matches `best_actions()` | TRAIN-06 |
22	
23	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
24	```python
25	def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]:
26	    configs = load_run_configs(config_path, [seed])
27	    plan = check_plan(configs)
28	    learners = train_checks(plan, seed)
29	    return score_checks(learners, plan)
30	```

35	**Covers:** TRAIN-01–TRAIN-04, TRAIN-07, LOG-01, LOG-02, CHECK-01, WORLD-01–WORLD-07, SNAKE-01–SNAKE-06, FISH-01–FISH-08, FISH-11, PREY-01–PREY-06, PREY-11, QR-01, QR-03, QR-06

57	## PL-03: Training batch
58	**Entry:** `src/rllab/train/pipelines/pl03_batch.py::run_batch` · **Trigger:** `rllab batch <config.toml> --seeds 1-5` (and `rllab train`, one seed)
59	**Covers:** TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, QR-02
60	
61	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
62	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
63	| 1 | `load_run_configs` | train.config | config path, seeds → list[RunConfig] | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-01 |
64	| 2 | `check_plan` | train.check | list[RunConfig] → MethodConfig per method | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
65	| 3 | `train_checks` | train.check | MethodConfig per method, seed → Learner per method | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
66	| 4 | `score_checks` | train.check | Learner per method, MethodConfig per method → list[CheckResult] | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
67	| 5 | `require_pass` | train.check | list[CheckResult] → list[CheckResult] | raise `CheckFailedError` on the first failure | TRAIN-06 |
68	| 6 | `run_parallel` | train.batch | list[RunConfig], root, workers → list[RunRecord] | `spawn` process pool, one `run_train` (PL-02 as built) per config, one torch thread each (AD-05) | TRAIN-05, QR-02 |
69	
70	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
71	```python
72	def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]:
73	    configs = load_run_configs(config_path, seeds)
74	    plan = check_plan(configs)
75	    learners = train_checks(plan, check_seed)
76	    results = score_checks(learners, plan)
77	    require_pass(results)
78	    return run_parallel(configs, root, workers)
79	```

113	```python

138	## PL-08: Replay video
139	**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipelines/pl08_video.py::run_video` · **Trigger:** `rllab video <world> <variant> <side> --seed N --out FILE [--sight]`
140	**Covers:** REPLAY-07, REPLAY-02, QR-06
141	
142	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
143	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
144	| 1 | `open_sources` | replay.sources | world, variant, side spec → Checkpoint or baseline per team | as `open_sides`, one side | REPLAY-07 |
145	| 2 | `make` | worlds.make | world, variant → World | PL-02 as built | WORLD-06 |
146	| 3 | `policies_for` | replay.sources | Checkpoint or baseline per team, WorldSpec → Policy per team | `methods.load_policy` or `baselines` per team; a team left out takes the first checkpoint's | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06 |
147	| 4 | `play_episode` | core.episode | World, Policy per team, seed → Episode | reset, act per team, step until done or `max_steps`, frames kept | REPLAY-02, WORLD-02 |
148	| 5 | `to_trace` | replay.tracing | Episode, WorldSpec, label → Trace | copy frames, set label and step-limit flag | REPLAY-02 |
149	| 6 | `save_video` | replay.video | Trace, out → Output | draw each frame off-screen, imageio-ffmpeg H.264 at 30 fps | REPLAY-07, QR-06 |
150	
151	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
152	```python
153	def run_video(world: str, variant: str, side: str, seed: int, out: Path, *, overlay: bool = False) -> Output:
154	    sources = open_sources(world, variant, side)
155	    w = make(world, variant)
156	    policies = policies_for(sources, w.spec)
157	    episode = play_episode(w, policies, seed, trace=True)
158	    trace = to_trace(episode, w.spec, side)
159	    return save_video(trace, out, overlay_sight=overlay)
160	```

162	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:9 -->
163	### core.ActionSpace: type
164	`ActionSpace = Discrete(n: int) | Box(dims: int, low: float, high: float)`, both frozen dataclasses
165	- **Invariants:** `Discrete` actions are ints in `[0, n)`; `Box` actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`
166	- **Boundary:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** snake `Discrete(3)`; fish `Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)`
167	- **Used by:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck
168	
169	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:15 -->
170	### core.TeamSpec: type
171	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: name: str; agents: tuple[int, ...]; score_name: str; direction: Direction`
172	- **Invariants:** `agents` are indices into the world's agent axis; teams of one world are disjoint and cover every agent
173	- **Boundary:** worlds, train, evaluate, charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** snake's one team; predator-prey's prey (6) and predator (2) teams
174	- **Used by:** every component reading scores
175	
176	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:21 -->
177	### core.WorldSpec: type
178	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class WorldSpec: world: str; variant: str; agents: tuple[str, ...]; teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]; obs_len: int; action: ActionSpace; max_steps: int; measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]; sight: float | None`
179	`MeasureSpec(name: str, team: str, direction: Direction)`
180	- **Invariants:** available without running an episode (WORLD-04); `sight` is the radius in world units for local-sight variants, else `None`; equality of two specs is field by field
181	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, runlog, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WorldSpec/ · **Cases:** snake default; fish local-sight (8 agents, sight set); predator-prey
182	- **Used by:** all components; PL-02, PL-08

185	### core.Step: type
186	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: Array; rewards: Array; active: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool`
187	- **Invariants:** `obs` float32 `n_agents × obs_len`; `rewards` float32 `n_agents`; `active` bool `n_agents`, and an inactive agent's row is zeros with reward 0 (WORLD edge case); `truncated` is true only when `max_steps` ended the episode
188	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a snake mid-episode step; a predator-prey step with one prey caught

220	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:59 -->
221	### core.World: type
222	```python
223	class World(Protocol):
224	    spec: WorldSpec
225	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...
226	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...
227	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...
228	    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...
229	```
230	- **Invariants:** same seed → same start (WORLD-01); same seed and actions → same steps (WORLD-02); `actions` has one row per agent, rows of inactive agents ignored
231	- **Errors:** `step` raises `ActionError(agent: str, value: object)`, a `ValueError`, for an action outside `spec.action`, never clipping it; `step` after `done` raises `RuntimeError`
232	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.World/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42
233	- **Used by:** every component that plays episodes; PL-02, PL-08

236	### core.Policy: type
237	`class Policy(Protocol): def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array`
238	- **Invariants:** `obs` is `k × obs_len` for the `k` agents of one team; returns `k` actions inside the world's `ActionSpace`; deterministic for a given `obs` and `rng` state
239	- **Boundary:** baselines, methods, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Policy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule policy over 5 observations
240	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08

243	### core.play_episode: The one episode loop
244	`def play_episode(world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False) -> Episode`
245	- **Invariants:** `policies` keyed by team; the seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05), so the same inputs give the same `Episode`; stops at `done` or `spec.max_steps`
246	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a team with no policy; `ActionError` propagates from `World.step`
247	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.play_episode/ · **Cases:** snake rule policy, seed 7, traced
248	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-02, PL-08

334	### methods.Learner: type
335	```python
336	class Learner(Protocol):
337	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...   # exploring
338	    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...                       # updates on its own schedule
339	    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...                                      # frozen, deterministic copy
340	    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...
341	```
342	- **Invariants:** hand-written learning code only (CHECK-01); `policy()` is greedy (DQN) or the mean action (PPO) and does not change as the learner trains
343	- **Errors:** `observe` raises `NonFiniteError(value: float)` when a loss or parameter is NaN or infinite, before changing any parameter
344	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Learner/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions
345	- **Used by:** train; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03

350	- **Invariants:** `dqn` needs a `Discrete` action space and `ppo` a `Box` one
351	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the method and the action format on a mismatch
352	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.make_learner/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded DQN learner double

368	`def load_run_configs(path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int]) -> list[RunConfig]`

375	### train.run_check: Method check
376	`def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]`
377	- **Invariants:** one result per distinct team method in the config, trained on its check world (`check-discrete` for `dqn`, `check-continuous` for `ppo`) with `check_settings`, for the fixed check budget; under a minute on the laptop (TRAIN-06, AD-09)
378	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing DQN check
379	- **Used by:** cli; PL-01

381	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:215 -->
382	### train.run_train: One training run
383	`def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord`
384	- **Invariants:** exactly `budget` world steps (TRAIN-01); checkpoints at 0%, 10%, … 100% (TRAIN-03); evaluation of every team on `EVAL_SEEDS` at every 5% (TRAIN-04); same config on the same machine → identical logs and checkpoints (TRAIN-02)
385	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` before any work if the run folder exists; `NonFiniteError` ends the run as `failed` with step and value, keeping the last good checkpoint; interruption marks it `incomplete`
386	- **Boundary:** PL-03 · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_train/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
387	- **Used by:** train.run_batch; PL-02, PL-03
388	
389	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:222 -->
390	### train.run_batch: Training batch
391	`def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]`
392	- **Invariants:** loads one config per seed, runs the method check with `check_seed` before any run, then one process per config up to `workers` (default: the core count), each with one torch thread (TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, AD-05)
393	- **Errors:** `CheckFailedError(result: CheckResult)` before any run starts; a failing run doesn't stop the others
394	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_batch/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 2 finished snake records
395	- **Used by:** cli; PL-03

492	### evaluate.Comparison: type
493	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]; team: str; learned_mean: float; baseline_mean: float; difference: float; p_raw: float; p_holm: float; rejected: bool`
494	- **Invariants:** `difference` is signed so positive means better in the team's direction
495	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Comparison/ · **Cases:** a rejected snake-vs-random; a kept snake-vs-rule
496	- **Used by:** evaluate.Summary
497	
498	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:330 -->
499	### evaluate.compare: Paired permutation test
500	`def compare(learned: Array, baseline: Array, direction: Direction, *, permutations: int = 10_000, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[float, float]`
501	- **Invariants:** `learned` is runs × seeds, averaged over runs per seed; `baseline` has one score per seed, on the same seeds; swaps labels within each seed; returns (difference, one-sided p) as EVAL-04 states; built and checked with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill
502	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if seed counts differ
503	- **Used by:** evaluate.final_evaluation
504	
505	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:336 -->
506	### evaluate.Summary: type
507	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Summary: world: str; variant: str; team: str; runs: tuple[str, ...]; learned_mean: float; learned_range: tuple[float, float]; run_means: Mapping[str, float]; random_mean: float; rule_mean: float; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; verdict: Verdict; collapse: int | None`
508	`Verdict(beats_baselines: bool, failing_runs: tuple[str, ...], runs_needed: int)`
509	- **Invariants:** one per world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08); `comparisons` empty and `runs_needed > 0` with fewer than 5 runs; `collapse` is set when the team's in-training eval score stays at or below its random-baseline mean for the last 4+ consecutive eval points, and is the step of the first of them
510	- **Boundary:** charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Summary/ · **Cases:** a passing snake summary; one with a failing run; one with 3 runs
511	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
512	
513	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:343 -->
514	### evaluate.final_evaluation: Final evaluation
515	`def final_evaluation(runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]`
516	- **Invariants:** plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS` (EVAL-03); Holm across the 2 comparisons per team; a pass needs both nulls rejected (EVAL-04) and every run's mean beating the rule mean (EVAL-05); in predator-prey each team plays against the rule-based opponent (PREY-08); within 10 minutes (QR-05)
517	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.final_evaluation/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
518	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
519	
520	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:466 -->
521	### cli.main: The rllab command
522	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
523	- **Invariants:** subcommands `check`, `train`, `batch`, `watch`, `chart`, `evaluate`, `grid`, `refcheck`, `replay`, `video`, `notes new`, `notes check`, each dispatched to its pipeline entry (`train` is a one-seed `batch`); prints the entry's returned contract type; owns no pipeline step (AD-10); every error is printed as one line naming its cause, exit code 1
524	- **Used by:** the learner
525

56	## Review
57	Run by the reviewer agent over what it is given (base, package, tasks, paths): the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
58	
59	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
60	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
61	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
62	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistically shaped, never carrying a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows; fakes serve their fixtures.
63	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
64	
65	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
66	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
67	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
68	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
69	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
70	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
71	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
72	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double (flagged for the system phase); fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
73	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
74	
75	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
76	
77	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.

1	# rllab — Contract
2	
3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)
4	
5	`Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]`, `Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]` and `Array = np.ndarray` are used throughout; `core` defines all three.
6	
7	## core
8	
9	### core.ActionSpace: type
10	`ActionSpace = Discrete(n: int) | Box(dims: int, low: float, high: float)`, both frozen dataclasses
11	- **Invariants:** `Discrete` actions are ints in `[0, n)`; `Box` actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`
12	- **Boundary:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** snake `Discrete(3)`; fish `Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)`
13	- **Used by:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck
14	
15	### core.TeamSpec: type
16	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: name: str; agents: tuple[int, ...]; score_name: str; direction: Direction`
17	- **Invariants:** `agents` are indices into the world's agent axis; teams of one world are disjoint and cover every agent
18	- **Boundary:** worlds, train, evaluate, charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** snake's one team; predator-prey's prey (6) and predator (2) teams
19	- **Used by:** every component reading scores
20	
21	### core.WorldSpec: type
22	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class WorldSpec: world: str; variant: str; agents: tuple[str, ...]; teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]; obs_len: int; action: ActionSpace; max_steps: int; measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]; sight: float | None`
23	`MeasureSpec(name: str, team: str, direction: Direction)`
24	- **Invariants:** available without running an episode (WORLD-04); `sight` is the radius in world units for local-sight variants, else `None`; equality of two specs is field by field
25	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, runlog, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WorldSpec/ · **Cases:** snake default; fish local-sight (8 agents, sight set); predator-prey
26	- **Used by:** all components; PL-02, PL-08
27	
28	### core.Step: type
29	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: Array; rewards: Array; active: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool`
30	- **Invariants:** `obs` float32 `n_agents × obs_len`; `rewards` float32 `n_agents`; `active` bool `n_agents`, and an inactive agent's row is zeros with reward 0 (WORLD edge case); `truncated` is true only when `max_steps` ended the episode
31	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a snake mid-episode step; a predator-prey step with one prey caught
32	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, refcheck
33	
34	### core.EpisodeResult: type
35	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EpisodeResult: seed: int; length: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
36	- **Invariants:** `scores` and `team_rewards` keyed by team name (WORLD-05, LOG-02); `measures` keyed by every `MeasureSpec.name`
37	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EpisodeResult/ · **Cases:** a snake episode with 12 food; a predator-prey episode with both team scores
38	- **Used by:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck
39	
40	### core.DrawState: type
41	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DrawState: width: float; height: float; objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]`
42	`Drawable(kind: str, x: float, y: float, size: float, team: str | None, agent: int | None)`
43	- **Invariants:** lists every object on screen at that step (WORLD-06); snake cells use grid coordinates with `size` 1; `agent` set for agents, so the viewer can draw `WorldSpec.sight` around them
44	- **Boundary:** worlds, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.DrawState/ · **Cases:** a snake board; an 8-fish tank with the ball
45	- **Used by:** replay
46	
47	### core.Frame: type
48	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: step: int; draw: DrawState; scores: Mapping[str, float]; rewards: Array`
49	- **Invariants:** `scores` is each team's running score after this step; `rewards` the latest per agent (REPLAY-05); frame 0 is the reset state with zero rewards
50	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** frame 0 and frame 1 of seed 7 snake
51	- **Used by:** replay
52	
53	### core.Episode: type
54	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Episode: result: EpisodeResult; frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None; hit_step_limit: bool`
55	- **Invariants:** `frames` is set only when traced, with `result.length + 1` entries
56	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Episode/ · **Cases:** an untraced snake episode; a traced 3-step check-world episode
57	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
58	
59	### core.World: type
60	```python
61	class World(Protocol):
62	    spec: WorldSpec
63	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...
64	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...
65	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...
66	    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...
67	```
68	- **Invariants:** same seed → same start (WORLD-01); same seed and actions → same steps (WORLD-02); `actions` has one row per agent, rows of inactive agents ignored
69	- **Errors:** `step` raises `ActionError(agent: str, value: object)`, a `ValueError`, for an action outside `spec.action`, never clipping it; `step` after `done` raises `RuntimeError`
70	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.World/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42
71	- **Used by:** every component that plays episodes; PL-02, PL-08
72	
73	### core.Policy: type
74	`class Policy(Protocol): def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array`
75	- **Invariants:** `obs` is `k × obs_len` for the `k` agents of one team; returns `k` actions inside the world's `ActionSpace`; deterministic for a given `obs` and `rng` state
76	- **Boundary:** baselines, methods, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Policy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule policy over 5 observations
77	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
78	
79	### core.play_episode: The one episode loop
80	`def play_episode(world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False) -> Episode`
81	- **Invariants:** `policies` keyed by team; the seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05), so the same inputs give the same `Episode`; stops at `done` or `spec.max_steps`
82	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a team with no policy; `ActionError` propagates from `World.step`
83	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.play_episode/ · **Cases:** snake rule policy, seed 7, traced
84	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-02, PL-08
85	
86	### core.EVAL_SEEDS: In-training evaluation seeds
87	`EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_020))`
88	- **Invariants:** 20 seeds, disjoint from training seeds `[0, 10⁶)` and from `FINAL_SEEDS` (TRAIN-04)
89	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EVAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 20 seeds as listed
90	- **Used by:** train, evaluate; PL-02, PL-05, PL-12
91	
92	### core.FINAL_SEEDS: Final evaluation seeds
93	`FINAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(2_000_000, 2_000_100))`
94	- **Invariants:** 100 seeds, shared by every run, baseline and reference (EVAL-03, CHECK-02)
95	- **Boundary:** evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.FINAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 100 seeds as listed
96	- **Used by:** evaluate, refcheck; PL-06, PL-09
97	
98	### core.Output: type
99	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Output: path: Path; kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]; made_from: tuple[str, ...]`
100	- **Invariants:** `path` exists when returned; `made_from` names the runs, checkpoints and seeds behind the file, so a write-up can cite it (NOTES-02)
101	- **Boundary:** charts, replay, notes, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Output/ · **Cases:** a 5-run snake learning-curve PNG; a seed-7 snake video
102	- **Used by:** charts, replay, notes, cli; PL-05, PL-08, PL-10, PL-12
103	
104	### core.MethodConfig: type
105	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class MethodConfig: method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]; settings: Settings`
106	- **Invariants:** `settings` holds only keys the method declares; missing keys take the method's defaults, filled by `train.load_run_configs` and written back in full to the run record; lives in `core` so `runlog` can hold it (AD-12)
107	- **Boundary:** methods, train, runlog, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MethodConfig/ · **Cases:** snake DQN defaults; PPO with one overridden learning rate
108	- **Used by:** methods, train, runlog, refcheck; PL-01, PL-03
109	
110	### core.RunConfig: type
111	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunConfig: name: str; world: str; variant: str; methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]; seed: int; budget: int; check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]; device: str = "cpu"`
112	- **Invariants:** `methods` has one entry per team (TRAIN-07); `budget` counts world steps, not agent steps; `check_settings` overrides settings for the check world only (AD-09); lives in `core` so `runlog` can hold it (AD-12)
113	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunConfig/ · **Cases:** a snake config from `configs/snake-default.toml`; a predator-prey config with two methods
114	- **Used by:** train, runlog, refcheck, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-09
115	
116	## worlds
117	
118	### worlds.make: World factory
119	`def make(world: str, variant: str = "default", **overrides: int | float) -> World`
120	- **Invariants:** worlds `snake` (`default`), `fish` (`one`, `many`, `local`), `prey` (`default`), `check-discrete`, `check-continuous`; `overrides` change only the spec's starting values (grid size, counts, step limits; spec Assumptions)
121	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming an unknown world, variant or override
122	- **Boundary:** baselines, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/worlds.make/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded seed-42 snake double for `make("snake")`
123	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck, baselines; PL-02, PL-08
124	
125	### worlds.CheckWorld: type
126	`class CheckWorld(World, Protocol): def states(self) -> Array; def best_actions(self) -> Array`
127	- **Invariants:** `states()` is the observation of every check-world state, `n × obs_len`; `best_actions()` the hand-derived best action for each, to within 0.1 per dimension for `Box` actions
128	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/worlds.CheckWorld/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the 6-state two-exit corridor; one of the 1-D target point
129	- **Used by:** train; PL-01

131	## baselines
132	
133	### baselines.RandomPolicy: Uniform random play
134	`class RandomPolicy(Policy): def __init__(self, action: ActionSpace) -> None`
135	- **Invariants:** each action drawn uniformly from the declared action format (EVAL-01), using only the `rng` passed to `act`
136	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomPolicy/ · **Cases:** 5 snake actions from a fixed `rng`; 5 fish pushes from a fixed `rng`
137	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay; PL-05
138	
139	### baselines.RulePolicy: type
140	`class RulePolicy(Policy, Protocol): description: str`
141	- **Invariants:** acts on observations only; `description` is the world's one sentence (EVAL-02)
142	- **Boundary:** evaluate, replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RulePolicy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule over 5 observations; the fish rule seeing no ball
143	- **Used by:** evaluate, replay, cli; PL-05
144	
145	### baselines.rule_for: Rule baseline lookup
146	`def rule_for(world: str, variant: str, team: str) -> RulePolicy`
147	- **Invariants:** SNAKE-07, FISH-09 and PREY-07 rules; deterministic except FISH-09's and PREY-07's random fallbacks, which use the `rng` passed to `act`
148	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a world, variant or team with no rule
149	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.rule_for/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded snake rule double
150	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay
151	
152	### baselines.BaselineMeans: type
153	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class BaselineMeans: world: str; variant: str; random: Mapping[str, float]; rule: Mapping[str, float]`
154	- **Invariants:** `random` and `rule` keyed by team; means over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes, the seeds the in-training curve uses; in predator-prey each team's baselines play the rule-based opponent
155	- **Boundary:** charts, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.BaselineMeans/ · **Cases:** snake; predator-prey with both teams
156	- **Used by:** charts, cli; PL-05
157	
158	### baselines.baseline_means: Baseline score lines
159	`def baseline_means(world: str, variant: str) -> BaselineMeans`
160	- **Invariants:** plays `RandomPolicy` and `rule_for` per team through `core.play_episode`; deterministic
161	- **Errors:** `KeyError` from `rule_for`
162	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.baseline_means/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake `BaselineMeans`
163	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
164	
165	## methods
166	
167	### methods.Transition: type
168	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: Array; actions: Array; rewards: Array; next_obs: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool; active: Array`
169	- **Invariants:** rows are the team's agents in `TeamSpec.agents` order; `active` masks agents that had already left before this step
170	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Transition/ · **Cases:** a snake transition; a many-fish transition of 8 agents
171	- **Used by:** train
172	
173	### methods.Learner: type
174	```python
175	class Learner(Protocol):
176	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...   # exploring
177	    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...                       # updates on its own schedule
178	    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...                                      # frozen, deterministic copy
179	    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...
180	```
181	- **Invariants:** hand-written learning code only (CHECK-01); `policy()` is greedy (DQN) or the mean action (PPO) and does not change as the learner trains
182	- **Errors:** `observe` raises `NonFiniteError(value: float)` when a loss or parameter is NaN or infinite, before changing any parameter
183	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Learner/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions
184	- **Used by:** train; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03
185	
186	### methods.make_learner: Learner factory
187	`def make_learner(config: MethodConfig, spec: WorldSpec, team: str, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Learner`
188	- **Invariants:** `dqn` needs a `Discrete` action space and `ppo` a `Box` one
189	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the method and the action format on a mismatch
190	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.make_learner/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded DQN learner double
191	- **Used by:** train; PL-02
192	
193	### methods.load_policy: Checkpoint to policy
194	`def load_policy(ckpt: Checkpoint, team: str, spec: WorldSpec) -> Policy`
195	- **Invariants:** the same checkpoint and team give a policy that acts identically every time
196	- **Errors:** `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing field (observation length, action format) when `ckpt.spec` doesn't match `spec` (REPLAY edge case)
197	- **Boundary:** evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.load_policy/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake rule policy for any snake checkpoint
198	- **Used by:** evaluate, replay; PL-08
199	
200	## train
201	
202	### train.load_run_configs: Config loader
203	`def load_run_configs(path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int]) -> list[RunConfig]`
204	- **Invariants:** one config per seed, named `<file stem>-s<seed>`; method settings missing from the TOML take the method's defaults
205	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` for a missing file; `ValueError` naming an unknown key, world, variant or method
206	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.load_run_configs/ · **Cases:** a fake returning snake configs for seeds 1 and 2
207	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-01, PL-03, PL-09
208	
209	### train.run_check: Method check
210	`def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]`
211	- **Invariants:** one result per distinct team method in the config, trained on its check world (`check-discrete` for `dqn`, `check-continuous` for `ppo`) with `check_settings`, for the fixed check budget; under a minute on the laptop (TRAIN-06, AD-09)
212	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing DQN check
213	- **Used by:** cli; PL-01
214	
215	### train.run_train: One training run
216	`def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord`
217	- **Invariants:** exactly `budget` world steps (TRAIN-01); checkpoints at 0%, 10%, … 100% (TRAIN-03); evaluation of every team on `EVAL_SEEDS` at every 5% (TRAIN-04); same config on the same machine → identical logs and checkpoints (TRAIN-02)
218	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` before any work if the run folder exists; `NonFiniteError` ends the run as `failed` with step and value, keeping the last good checkpoint; interruption marks it `incomplete`
219	- **Boundary:** PL-03 · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_train/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
220	- **Used by:** train.run_batch; PL-02, PL-03
221	
222	### train.run_batch: Training batch
223	`def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]`
224	- **Invariants:** loads one config per seed, runs the method check with `check_seed` before any run, then one process per config up to `workers` (default: the core count), each with one torch thread (TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, AD-05)
225	- **Errors:** `CheckFailedError(result: CheckResult)` before any run starts; a failing run doesn't stop the others
226	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_batch/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 2 finished snake records
227	- **Used by:** cli; PL-03
228	
229	### train.CheckResult: type
230	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CheckResult: method: str; world: str; agreement: float; passed: bool`
231	- **Invariants:** `agreement` is the share of check-world states where the trained policy picks the best action; `passed` is `agreement >= 0.95` (TRAIN-06)
232	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.CheckResult/ · **Cases:** a passing DQN check; a failing PPO check at 0.80
233	- **Used by:** cli; PL-01, PL-03
234	
235	## runlog
236	
237	### runlog.RunRecord: type
238	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: config: RunConfig; spec: WorldSpec; started: datetime; ended: datetime | None; status: Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]; failure: Failure | None; path: Path`
239	`Failure(step: int, value: float, checkpoint: Path)`
240	- **Invariants:** every LOG-01 field present once the run ends; `config` is a `core.RunConfig` and `config.methods` holds full settings, defaults included; every field type comes from `core`, so `runlog` imports `core` only (AD-12)
241	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a finished snake run; a failed run with a NaN at step 41,200; an incomplete run
242	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, charts, refcheck, cli; PL-02, PL-03
243	
244	### runlog.EvalPoint: type
245	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalPoint: step: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
246	- **Invariants:** means over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes, per team and per measure; a finished run has 21, at 0%, 5%, … 100% (TRAIN-04)
247	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.EvalPoint/ · **Cases:** snake point at 50%; predator-prey point with both teams
248	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, cli
249	
250	### runlog.Checkpoint: type
251	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Checkpoint: run: str; pct: int; step: int; spec: WorldSpec; methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]; state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]; path: Path`
252	- **Invariants:** `pct` in {0, 10, …, 100}; `methods` holds `core.MethodConfig`s (AD-12); `state` keyed by team; saved and loaded through `torch.save`/`torch.load(weights_only=True)`
253	- **Boundary:** methods, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Checkpoint/ · **Cases:** the 0% and 100% checkpoints of a short snake run
254	- **Used by:** methods, evaluate, replay; PL-07, PL-08
255	
256	### runlog.Run: type
257	```python
258	class Run(Protocol):
259	    record: RunRecord
260	    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...
261	    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...
262	    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...
263	```
264	- **Invariants:** read-only view of one run folder; readable while the run writes (LOG-06)
265	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, replay, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Run/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
266	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, replay, refcheck, cli; PL-05, PL-06, PL-12
267	
268	### runlog.RunWriter: Run folder writer
269	```python
270	class RunWriter:
271	    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None: ...
272	    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None: ...
273	    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None: ...      # flushes every log
274	    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint: ...
275	    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord: ...
276	```
277	- **Invariants:** `config` is a `core.RunConfig` (AD-12)
278	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` naming the path if `runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>` exists (TRAIN edge case)
279	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the writes of a 3-checkpoint snake run
280	- **Used by:** train; PL-02
281	
282	### runlog.find_runs: Run lookup
283	`def find_runs(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()) -> list[Run]`
284	- **Invariants:** finished runs only, plus incomplete or failed runs named in `include` (TRAIN edge case); a `running` run with no live process is reported as incomplete
285	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.find_runs/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 5 finished snake runs plus one named incomplete run
286	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, refcheck, cli; PL-05, PL-06
287	
288	### runlog.open_run: Open one run
289	`def open_run(path: Path) -> Run`
290	- **Invariants:** any status, including `running`, `incomplete` and `failed`
291	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if no `run.json` is at `path`
292	- **Boundary:** replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.open_run/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the finished 3-checkpoint snake run double
293	- **Used by:** replay, cli; PL-12
294	
295	### runlog.Progress: type
296	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Progress: step: int; budget: int; latest: EvalPoint | None; elapsed: timedelta; status: str`
297	- **Invariants:** at most one evaluation point behind the run (LOG-06)
298	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Progress/ · **Cases:** a run at 35% with the 35% point written
299	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04

302	`def progress(path: Path) -> Progress`
303	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if no run is at `path`

307	## charts
308	
309	### charts.learning_curve: Learning-curve chart
310	`def learning_curve(runs: Sequence[Run], baselines: BaselineMeans, out: Path, *, measure: str | None = None) -> Output`
311	- **Invariants:** draws per team a mean line, a min–max band and both baseline lines against "environment steps" (LOG-03, PREY-10), or a declared measure the same way (LOG-05); follows the `dataviz` skill (LOG-04); under 3 runs, titled with the count and a band note
312	- **Errors:** `MixedRunsError` naming the differing settings when runs differ by more than seed
313	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/charts.learning_curve/ · **Cases:** a fake writing a placeholder PNG for 5 snake runs
314	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
315	
316	### charts.pairing_heatmap: Pairing-grid chart
317	`def pairing_heatmap(grid: PairingGrid, out: Path) -> Output`
318	- **Invariants:** one 11 × 11 heatmap per team, prey checkpoints on one axis and predator checkpoints on the other; follows the `dataviz` skill
319	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/charts.pairing_heatmap/ · **Cases:** a fake writing a placeholder PNG for the planted grid
320	- **Used by:** cli; PL-12
321	
322	## evaluate
323	
324	### evaluate.Comparison: type
325	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]; team: str; learned_mean: float; baseline_mean: float; difference: float; p_raw: float; p_holm: float; rejected: bool`
326	- **Invariants:** `difference` is signed so positive means better in the team's direction
327	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Comparison/ · **Cases:** a rejected snake-vs-random; a kept snake-vs-rule
328	- **Used by:** evaluate.Summary
329	
330	### evaluate.compare: Paired permutation test
331	`def compare(learned: Array, baseline: Array, direction: Direction, *, permutations: int = 10_000, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[float, float]`
332	- **Invariants:** `learned` is runs × seeds, averaged over runs per seed; `baseline` has one score per seed, on the same seeds; swaps labels within each seed; returns (difference, one-sided p) as EVAL-04 states; built and checked with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill
333	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if seed counts differ
334	- **Used by:** evaluate.final_evaluation
335	
336	### evaluate.Summary: type
337	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Summary: world: str; variant: str; team: str; runs: tuple[str, ...]; learned_mean: float; learned_range: tuple[float, float]; run_means: Mapping[str, float]; random_mean: float; rule_mean: float; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; verdict: Verdict; collapse: int | None`
338	`Verdict(beats_baselines: bool, failing_runs: tuple[str, ...], runs_needed: int)`
339	- **Invariants:** one per world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08); `comparisons` empty and `runs_needed > 0` with fewer than 5 runs; `collapse` is set when the team's in-training eval score stays at or below its random-baseline mean for the last 4+ consecutive eval points, and is the step of the first of them
340	- **Boundary:** charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Summary/ · **Cases:** a passing snake summary; one with a failing run; one with 3 runs
341	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
342	
343	### evaluate.final_evaluation: Final evaluation
344	`def final_evaluation(runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]`
345	- **Invariants:** plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS` (EVAL-03); Holm across the 2 comparisons per team; a pass needs both nulls rejected (EVAL-04) and every run's mean beating the rule mean (EVAL-05); in predator-prey each team plays against the rule-based opponent (PREY-08); within 10 minutes (QR-05)
346	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.final_evaluation/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
347	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
348	
349	### evaluate.load_summaries: Saved summaries
350	`def load_summaries(root: Path, world: str, variant: str) -> list[Summary]`
351	- **Invariants:** the latest `_eval/<date>/summary.json` for that world and variant, one `Summary` per team
352	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming `rllab evaluate` when none exists
353	- **Boundary:** refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.load_summaries/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
354	- **Used by:** refcheck; PL-09
355	
356	### evaluate.PairingGrid: type
357	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PairingGrid: run: str; prey_pcts: tuple[int, ...]; predator_pcts: tuple[int, ...]; scores: Mapping[str, Array]`
358	- **Invariants:** `scores` keyed by team, each 11 × 11 (one row and column per checkpoint), cell [i, j] the mean of 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes of prey checkpoint i against predator checkpoint j (PREY-09)
359	- **Boundary:** charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.PairingGrid/ · **Cases:** an 11 × 11 grid with planted values
360	- **Used by:** charts; PL-12
361	
362	### evaluate.pairing_grid: Checkpoint pairings
363	`def pairing_grid(run: Run, *, workers: int | None = None) -> PairingGrid`
364	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the run's world doesn't have exactly two teams
365	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.pairing_grid/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the planted 11 × 11 grid
366	- **Used by:** cli; PL-12
367	
368	## replay
369	
370	### replay.Trace: type
371	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Trace: label: str; spec: WorldSpec; seed: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; hit_step_limit: bool`
372	- **Invariants:** same sources and seed → equal frames (REPLAY-02); `label` names the checkpoint or baseline per team
373	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Trace/ · **Cases:** the rule snake on seed 7
374	- **Used by:** replay.view, replay.save_video; PL-07, PL-08
375	
376	### replay.open_sides: Replay sides
377	`def open_sides(world: str, variant: str, sides: Sequence[str]) -> list[Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]]`
378	- **Invariants:** one or two sides, each `<run-path>@<pct>`, `random` or `rule`; predator-prey sides may name each team (`prey=<run>@<pct>,predator=<run>@<pct>`); checkpoints read through `runlog.open_run`
379	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming a malformed side or a third side; `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing field when a checkpoint's spec no longer matches the world (REPLAY edge case)
380	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_sides/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the `rule` side and a 100% snake checkpoint side
381	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
382	
383	### replay.open_sources: One replay side
384	`def open_sources(world: str, variant: str, side: str) -> Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]`
385	- **Invariants:** one side in `open_sides`'s format
386	- **Errors:** as `open_sides`
387	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_sources/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a 50% snake checkpoint side
388	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
389	
390	### replay.policies_for: Side to policies
391	`def policies_for(sources: Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]], spec: WorldSpec) -> Mapping[str, Policy]`
392	- **Invariants:** one policy per team of `spec`; a team left out takes its policy from the first checkpoint given, so one predator-prey checkpoint replays both teams (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06, PREY-09)
393	- **Errors:** `SpecMismatchError` from `methods.load_policy`
394	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.policies_for/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake rule policy
395	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
396	
397	### replay.to_trace: Episode to trace
398	`def to_trace(episode: Episode, spec: WorldSpec, label: str) -> Trace`
399	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `episode.frames` is `None`
400	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.to_trace/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rule-snake seed-7 trace
401	- **Used by:** cli, replay.trace_sides; PL-08
402	
403	### replay.trace_sides: Traces for each side
404	`def trace_sides(world: str, variant: str, sides: Sequence[Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]], seed: int) -> list[Trace]`
405	- **Invariants:** per side, a fresh world, `policies_for`, `core.play_episode` (traced) and `to_trace`; every side starts from the same seed (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-04)
406	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.trace_sides/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rule snake and random snake traces on seed 7
407	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
408	
409	### replay.Session: type
410	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Session: labels: tuple[str, ...]; last_step: tuple[int, ...]; speed: float; overlay_sight: bool`
411	- **Invariants:** one entry per trace shown; `last_step` is the frame each trace showed when the window closed; `speed` in {0.25, 1, 4, 16}
412	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Session/ · **Cases:** one trace closed at step 30; two traces with one ended early
413	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
414	
415	### replay.view: Replay window
416	`def view(traces: Sequence[Trace], *, overlay_sight: bool = False, keys: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Session`
417	- **Invariants:** one or two traces side by side, stepping in sync, a finished trace holding its last frame (REPLAY-04); pause, step ±1 and 0.25×/1×/4×/16× where 1× is 30 steps/s (REPLAY-03, QR-04); step, scores and rewards shown (REPLAY-05); a sight toggle when `spec.sight` is set (REPLAY-08); "stopped at step limit" shown when `hit_step_limit`; with `keys`, plays those key presses in a hidden window and closes, so it runs headless in tests
418	- **Errors:** `ValueError` for more than two traces
419	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.view/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a `Session` for the traces and overlay flag it was given
420	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
421	
422	### replay.save_video: Replay to MP4
423	`def save_video(trace: Trace, out: Path, *, overlay_sight: bool = False) -> Output`
424	- **Invariants:** the whole episode at 1× (30 frames/s) as H.264 MP4 that plays in a standard player (REPLAY-07); offline (QR-06)
425	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.save_video/ · **Cases:** a fake writing an empty file at `out`
426	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
427	
428	## refcheck
429	
430	### refcheck.CheckReport: type
431	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CheckReport: world: str; variant: str; team: str; reference_mean: float; reference_range: tuple[float, float]; hand_mean: float; hand_range: tuple[float, float]; ratio: float; outcome: Literal["pass", "investigate-low", "investigate-high"]`
432	- **Invariants:** `ratio` is hand ÷ reference; for higher-is-better a pass is 0.8 ≤ ratio ≤ 1.5, and for lower-is-better 2/3 ≤ ratio ≤ 1.25 (CHECK-03, CHECK edge case)
433	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/refcheck.CheckReport/ · **Cases:** 18 vs 20 food (pass); 15 vs 20 (investigate-low)
434	- **Used by:** cli; PL-09
435	
436	### refcheck.reference_check: Reference implementation check
437	`def reference_check(configs: Sequence[RunConfig], root: Path) -> list[CheckReport]`
438	- **Invariants:** trains SB3 `DQN` or `PPO` for each config (same world, budget and seeds as the hand-written runs; AD-08) under `_reference/`, evaluates on `FINAL_SEEDS` (CHECK-02), and compares with `evaluate.load_summaries`; one report per team; the only code importing `stable_baselines3` or `gymnasium`
439	- **Errors:** `ImportError` naming the `check` extra when it isn't installed; `FileNotFoundError` from `load_summaries` when the hand-written runs have no final evaluation
440	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/refcheck.reference_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 18-vs-20 passing report
441	- **Used by:** cli; PL-09
442

446	`def scaffold(world: str, root: Path) -> Output`
447	- **Invariants:** writes `notes/<world>.md` with only the three part headings (NOTES-01, NOTES-03)
448	- **Errors:** `FileExistsError` if the write-up exists; never overwrites
449	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/notes.scaffold/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the three-heading write-up

454	- **Invariants:** `ok` only when no part is empty, the struggle part links at least one existing file under `reports/` (NOTES-02) and no link is broken (NOTES edge case)
455	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/notes.FinishReport/ · **Cases:** a complete write-up; one with a broken chart link

464	## cli
465	
466	### cli.main: The rllab command
467	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
468	- **Invariants:** subcommands `check`, `train`, `batch`, `watch`, `chart`, `evaluate`, `grid`, `refcheck`, `replay`, `video`, `notes new`, `notes check`, each dispatched to its pipeline entry (`train` is a one-seed `batch`); prints the entry's returned contract type; owns no pipeline step (AD-10); every error is printed as one line naming its cause, exit code 1
469	- **Used by:** the learner

107	# pytest exits 5 when it collects nothing. That is fine for gate and full on a project with no
108	# tests yet; collect calls pytest through run() so an empty tests/intent fails.
109	run_tests() {
110	  echo "-> pytest $*" >&2
111	  uv run --frozen --quiet pytest "$@"
112	  local status=$?
113	  [ "$status" -eq 5 ] && { echo "(no tests collected)" >&2; return; }
114	  [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || { rc=1; failed+=("pytest $*"); }

117	# The commit this task started from: refs/worktree/checks-base, set per worktree by `dt claim` (and
118	# by `dt land` in the main checkout). Without it, HEAD, which misses changes already committed.
119	BASE_REF="refs/worktree/checks-base"
120	base_commit() {
121	  git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD 2>/dev/null
122	}
123	
124	# A boundary contract is frozen once its folder exists at the base (CR-09): each folder under
125	# tests/contracts/ at the base, and any file directly in it. A folder new since the base is the
126	# interface task freezing it, so that task may write it; the next task's base freezes it. A frozen
127	# folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (from its Writes:, by dt claim) is a re-freeze
128	# interface task's, so it is open to that task; dt land checks the Writes: against the plan.
129	CONTRACTS_GLOB="tests/contracts/**"
130	claimed_contract() {
131	  local line
132	  [ -s .claude/.task_writes ] || return 1
133	  while IFS= read -r line; do
134	    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
135	    line="${line%/}"; line="${line%/\*\*}"
136	    [ "$line" = "$1" ] && return 0
137	  done <.claude/.task_writes
138	  return 1
139	}
140	frozen_contracts() {
141	  local base meta path
142	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
143	  git ls-tree "$base" -- tests/contracts/ 2>/dev/null | while IFS=$'\t' read -r meta path; do
144	    case "$meta" in
145	      *" tree "*) claimed_contract "$path" || echo "$path/**" ;;
146	      *) echo "$path" ;;
147	    esac
148	  done
149	}

151	# The protected list as enforced: PROTECTED, with tests/contracts/** narrowed to the frozen contracts.
152	protected_globs() {
153	  local g
154	  for g in "${PROTECTED[@]}"; do
155	    if [ "$g" = "$CONTRACTS_GLOB" ]; then frozen_contracts; else echo "$g"; fi
156	  done
157	}

159	# Fail if anything matching the given pathspecs changed since the base, untracked files included.
160	unchanged_since_base() {
161	  local label="$1" base new
162	  shift
163	  echo "-> $label unchanged since $(git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "$BASE_REF" || echo HEAD)" >&2
164	  if ! base=$(base_commit); then
165	    echo "no commit to compare against: commit the tester's work first" >&2
166	    rc=1; failed+=("$label: no commit"); return
167	  fi
168	  git --no-pager diff --exit-code --stat "$base" -- "$@" >&2 || { rc=1; failed+=("$label changed"); }
169	  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
170	  if [ -n "$new" ]; then
171	    printf 'new untracked files in %s:\n%s\n' "$label" "$new" >&2
172	    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
173	  fi
174	}

176	# The tester may change tests/intent only. Docs and plans are the plugin's business, not this check's.
177	only_intent_changed() {
178	  local specs=(tests "${SRC_DIRS[@]}" "${PROTECTED[@]}")
179	  [ -n "$PACKAGES_DIR" ] && specs+=("$PACKAGES_DIR")
180	  unchanged_since_base "files outside tests/intent" "${specs[@]}" ':(exclude)tests/intent'
181	}

184	changed_intent_tests() {

187	  { git diff --relative --name-only --diff-filter=d "$base" -- 'tests/intent/*.py'
188	    git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'tests/intent/*.py'; } | sort -u |
189	    while IFS= read -r f; do case "${f##*/}" in test_*|*_test.py) echo "$f" ;; esac; done
190	}
191	
192	# Test directories for this run: all of them, or the --pkg scope; tests/contracts always whole.
193	test_dirs() {
194	  local scope="" d p
195	  [ -n "$pkg" ] && scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
196	  for d in tests/intent tests/unit; do
197	    if [ -z "$scope" ]; then
198	      [ -d "$d" ] && echo "$d"
199	    else
200	      for p in $scope; do [ -d "$d/$p" ] && echo "$d/$p"; done
201	    fi
202	  done
203	  [ -d tests/contracts ] && echo tests/contracts
204	}
205	
206	# deptry checks what each package imports against what it declares.
207	dependencies() {
208	  local p scope
209	  if [ -z "$PACKAGES_DIR" ]; then
210	    run deptry . --no-ansi
211	    return
212	  fi
213	  if [ -n "$pkg" ]; then
214	    scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
215	  else
216	    scope=$(cd "$PACKAGES_DIR" && for d in */pyproject.toml; do [ -f "$d" ] && dirname "$d"; done)
217	  fi
218	  for p in $scope; do
219	    echo "-> deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p" >&2
220	    (cd "$PACKAGES_DIR/$p" && uv run --frozen --quiet deptry . --no-ansi) || { rc=1; failed+=("deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p"); }
221	  done
222	}
223	
224	static_checks() {
225	  run ruff check --ignore-noqa .   # exceptions go in per-file-ignores, which is protected (CR-01)
226	  run ruff format --check .
227	  run "$TYPE_GATE"
228	  run python scripts/check_size.py "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"
229	  dependencies
230	  run lint-imports --no-logo
231	}
232	
233	case "$mode" in
234	  fast)
235	    # Only Python files, only the files just edited. Fixes what it can in place.
236	    files=()
237	    for f in ${args[@]+"${args[@]}"}; do [[ $f == *.py ]] && files+=("$f"); done
238	    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0
239	    run ruff check --fix --quiet "${files[@]}"
240	    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
241	    run typos "${files[@]}"
242	    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
243	    ;;
244	  collect)
245	    if [ ! -d tests/intent ]; then
246	      echo "tests/intent does not exist: write the intent tests there" >&2
247	      exit 1
248	    fi
249	    run ruff check --ignore-noqa tests/intent
250	    run ruff format --check tests/intent
251	    run typos tests/intent
252	    run pytest tests/intent --collect-only -q --no-cov
253	    changed=()
254	    while IFS= read -r f; do changed+=("$f"); done < <(changed_intent_tests)
255	    run python scripts/red_check.py ${changed[@]+"${changed[@]}"}
256	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
257	    only_intent_changed
258	    ;;
259	  gate)
260	    static_checks
261	    other_parts
262	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
263	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086  # one directory per word, by construction
264	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && run_tests $dirs --no-cov -q -x
265	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
266	    protected=()
267	    while IFS= read -r g; do protected+=("$g"); done < <(protected_globs)
268	    unchanged_since_base "protected files" "${protected[@]}"
269	    ;;
270	  full)
271	    static_checks
272	    other_parts
273	    if [ "$TYPE_GATE" = mypy ]; then run pyright; else run mypy; fi
274	    # CR-41: the interface task's stubs and fields stay unused until later tasks use them.
275	    if [ ${#exit_check[@]} -gt 0 ]; then run vulture; else report vulture; fi
276	    run pylint --disable=all --enable=duplicate-code --recursive=y --score=n --fail-under=10 "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"   # CR-42
277	    run typos
278	    run pip-audit --skip-editable   # the external opinion: a vulnerability database (CR-26)
279	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh ${exit_check[@]+"${exit_check[@]}"}
280	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
281	    # Coverage threshold comes from [tool.coverage.report] fail_under (CR-40).
282	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
283	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci run_tests $dirs --cov -q
284	    ;;
285	  protected)
286	    protected_globs
287	    ;;
288	  *)
289	    echo "unknown mode: $mode (fast | collect | gate | full | protected)" >&2
290	    exit 64
291	    ;;
292	esac
293	[ $rc -ne 0 ] && printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "${failed[@]}" >&2
294	exit $rc

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Diff-scoped floor checks for CONSTRAINTS.md (CR-01 to CR-04, CR-07, CR-08).
3	
4	Called by scripts/floor_guard.sh, which writes the diff, the untracked-file list and the
5	base commit's pyproject.toml. Prints one line per finding and exits 1 if there are any:
6	
7	    path:line: [rule] message
8	
9	Rules (CONSTRAINTS.md ID in brackets):
10	    suppression [CR-01]            a new `type: ignore`, `pyright:`, `noqa`, `pragma: no cover`...
11	    stub [CR-02]                   a function whose body is only pass, ... or NotImplementedError
12	    stub-task [CR-02]              an interface stub, `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`, naming
13	                                   a task that is not open in tasks/todo.md (whole repo, not the diff)
14	    stub-left [CR-02]              any interface stub, with --exit (the milestone exit)
15	    swallowed-exception [CR-02]    `except:` / `except Exception:` with only pass
16	    test-skipped [CR-03]           a new skip, skipif, xfail or importorskip
17	    test-deleted [CR-03]           a test file removed
18	    assertion-removed [CR-03]      a test file lost more assertion lines than it gained
19	    test-without-assertion [CR-03] a changed test function that asserts nothing
20	    secret [CR-04]                 a credential pattern (the value is never printed)
21	    references-import [CR-08]      an import from the references folder
22	    references-path [CR-08]        the references folder put on an import path (sys.path,
23	                                   an environment variable, pytest or type-checker config)
24	    probes-import, probes-path     the same for the probes folder [CR-08]
25	    bar-loosened [CR-07]           pyproject.toml tool config weakened, or a new config file
26	    rule-removed [CR-07]           a CR rule gone from CONSTRAINTS.md
27	    new-exception [CR-07]          a new row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table
28	
29	An interface stub is the one placeholder body CR-02 allows: the milestone's interface task writes
30	each step signature that way, naming the task that replaces it, and testers import it before the
31	code exists. It is never a `stub` finding; `stub-task` and `stub-left` judge it instead.
32	
33	A CR-01 to CR-03 or CR-08 finding is allowed when an unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's
34	Exceptions table names that rule and a path glob matching the file. Secrets and CR-07 never are.
35	Files under scripts/ are skipped for code checks: they are the bar, protected separately.
36	Files under references/ and probes/ are inputs, not project code: only the secret check reads them.
37	"""

105	class Finding(NamedTuple):
106	    rule: str
107	    path: str
108	    line: int  # 0 when the finding has no single line
109	    message: str
110	
111	    def __str__(self) -> str:
112	        where = f"{self.path}:{self.line}" if self.line else self.path
113	        return f"{where}: [{self.rule}] {self.message}"

116	@dataclass
117	class Diff:
118	    added: dict[str, list[tuple[int, str]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
119	    removed: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
120	    deleted: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
121	
122	    def added_lines(self, path: str) -> set[int]:
123	        return {n for n, _ in self.added.get(path, [])}
124	
125	
126	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- the diff
127	HUNK = re.compile(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@")
128	
129	
130	def _diff_path(raw: str) -> str:
131	    raw = raw.rstrip("\t")
132	    return raw[2:] if raw[:2] in {"a/", "b/"} else raw
133	
134	
135	def parse_diff(text: str) -> Diff:
136	    """Added lines (with new line numbers), removed lines and deleted files, per file."""
137	    diff = Diff()
138	    old = new = ""
139	    line_no = 0
140	    in_header = False
141	    for line in text.splitlines():
142	        if line.startswith("diff --git "):
143	            old, new, in_header = "", "", True
144	        elif in_header and line.startswith("--- "):
145	            old = _diff_path(line[4:])
146	        elif in_header and line.startswith("+++ "):
147	            new = _diff_path(line[4:])
148	            if new == "/dev/null":
149	                diff.deleted.add(old)
150	        elif match := HUNK.match(line):
151	            in_header, line_no = False, int(match[1])
152	        elif in_header:
153	            continue
154	        elif line.startswith("+"):
155	            diff.added.setdefault(new, []).append((line_no, line[1:]))
156	            line_no += 1
157	        elif line.startswith("-"):
158	            diff.removed.setdefault(old, []).append(line[1:])
159	    return diff
160	
161	
162	def add_untracked(diff: Diff, paths: list[str]) -> None:
163	    """An untracked file is all added lines."""
164	    for name in paths:
165	        path = Path(name)
166	        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
167	            continue
168	        try:
169	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
170	        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
171	            continue
172	        diff.added[name] = list(enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1))
173	
174	
175	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- line checks
176	def is_outside(path: str) -> bool:
177	    return path.startswith(tuple(f"{folder}/" for folder in OUTSIDE))
178	
179	
180	def is_python(path: str) -> bool:
181	    if path.startswith("scripts/") or is_outside(path):
182	        return False
183	    return path.endswith((".py", ".pyi"))
184	
185	
186	def is_test_file(path: str) -> bool:
187	    parts = Path(path).parts
188	    name = parts[-1] if parts else ""
189	    return (
190	        "tests" in parts
191	        and (name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"))
192	        and not is_outside(path)
193	    )
194	
195	
196	def line_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
197	    found: list[Finding] = []
198	    for path, lines in diff.added.items():
199	        python, test = is_python(path), is_test_file(path)
200	        path_settings = not is_outside(path) and not path.endswith(DOC_SUFFIXES)
201	        for n, text in lines:
202	            if python and SUPPRESSION.search(text):
203	                found.append(Finding("suppression", path, n, "new suppression comment"))
204	            if path_settings and IMPORT_PATH_SETTING.search(text):
205	                found += [
206	                    Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, n, f"{folder}/ on an import path")
207	                    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items()
208	                    if word.search(text)
209	                ]
210	            if test and SKIP.search(text):
211	                found.append(Finding("test-skipped", path, n, "test skipped or marked xfail"))
212	            if Path(path).name not in LOCKFILES:
213	                found += [
214	                    Finding("secret", path, n, f"looks like a {kind} (value not shown)")
215	                    for kind, pattern in SECRETS.items()
216	                    if pattern.search(text)
217	                ]
218	    return found
219	
220	
221	def test_file_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
222	    found = [
223	        Finding("test-deleted", p, 0, "test file deleted") for p in diff.deleted if is_test_file(p)
224	    ]
225	    for path, lines in diff.removed.items():
226	        if not is_test_file(path) or path in diff.deleted:
227	            continue
228	        lost = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for t in lines)
229	        gained = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []))
230	        if lost > gained:
231	            found.append(
232	                Finding("assertion-removed", path, 0, f"{lost - gained} assertion line(s) removed")
233	            )
234	    return found
